              text: [32m'# Teach Docling a New Input Format (RCP/1.0)\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'## Background\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'Docling is preinstalled in this container (`import docling`, distribution `docling-slim` 2.115.0, `docling-core` 2.87.1, Python 3.12) together with all of its model weights. **The container has no network access**: nothing may be downloaded, and no external or cloud service may be contacted. Only preinstalled packages may be used.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'A lab publishes its protocols in a small in-house plain-text format called **RCP/1.0** (`.rcp`). Downstream teams want those protocols in the same document model, exports and chunk stream that Docling already produces for its natively supported formats, so RCP must become a first-class Docling input format rather than a side-channel parser.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'## The RCP/1.0 format\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'An RCP file is UTF-8 text with LF line endings and is read line by line.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'1. The **first line** must be exactly `%RCP/1.0`.\n'[39m +
                [32m'2. Then comes the **header block**: every line has the form `<key>=<value>`, where `<key>` matches `[a-z][a-z0-9_]*` and `<value>` is the rest of the line with surrounding whitespace stripped. The header block ends at the first line that is exactly `%%`. Keys `id` and `title` are required and must have a non-empty value; other keys are allowed and carry no meaning. A repeated key is an error.\n'[39m +
                [32m'3. Everything after the `%%` line is the **body**. Blank lines (empty or whitespace-only) carry no content; they only terminate the block they follow. Every other body line must be exactly one of:\n'[39m +
                [32m'   * `S<n>> <text>` with `<n>` in `1`..`6` — a section heading of level `<n>`.\n'[39m +
                [32m'   * `P> <text>` — a paragraph.\n'[39m +
                [32m'   * `N> <text>` — an ordered step. A run of consecutive `N>` lines is one ordered list.\n'[39m +
                [32m'   * `B> <text>` — a bullet. A run of consecutive `B>` lines is one unordered list.\n'[39m +
                [32m'   * `A> <text>` — an annotation.\n'[39m +
                [32m'   * `F> <ref> :: <caption>` — a figure reference with a caption.\n'[39m +
                [32m'   * A line starting with `|` — one table row. A run of consecutive table-row lines is one table. The cells of a row are obtained by dropping the leading `|`, then dropping one trailing `|` if present, then splitting on `|` and stripping each cell. The first row of a table is its column-header row, and every row of the same table must yield the same number of cells.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'A file that violates any rule above (bad magic line, malformed or unterminated header, repeated header key, missing/empty `id` or `title`, unrecognized body line, ragged table) is **malformed**.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'## Requirements\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'Fixtures live under `/home/user/project/corpus` (`clean/`, `mixed/`, `malformed/`, `unsupported/`); they are inputs, not templates for your output.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'### 1. RCP as a Docling input format\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m"Docling's own conversion entry point must handle `.rcp` inputs. Expose `build_converter()` in `/home/user/project/rcp_plugin.py`; it must return a `docling.document_converter.DocumentConverter` instance that:\n"[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'* converts `.rcp` documents given as a filesystem path **and** as an in-memory Docling document stream named `<something>.rcp`, reporting conversion success and returning a Docling document;\n'[39m +
                [32m'* still converts natively supported Markdown (`.md`) documents successfully through the same converter instance;\n'[39m +
                [32m'* reports a conversion **failure** status for a malformed `.rcp` file instead of letting an exception escape, when the caller asks for errors not to be raised;\n'[39m +
                [32m'* does **not** report success for any other input format (e.g. `.csv`).\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'A converted RCP document must carry the following structure:\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Its document name is the header `id` value.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Its body starts with a single title element whose text is the header `title` value.\n'[39m +
                [32m"* Each `S<n>>` line becomes a section-heading element whose heading level equals `<n>` and whose text is the line's text. A heading is nested inside the closest preceding heading of a smaller level (if any); every content element is nested inside the closest preceding heading.\n"[39m +
                [32m'* Each `P>` line becomes a plain text element with that text.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Each `A>` line becomes a plain text element whose text is exactly `NOTE: ` followed by the annotation text.\n'[39m +
                [32m"* Each run of `N>` lines becomes one enumerated (ordered) list, each run of `B>` lines one non-enumerated list, with one list item per line, in file order, carrying the line's text.\n"[39m +
                [32m'* Each table block becomes one table element with `num_rows` equal to the number of rows in the block, `num_cols` equal to the number of cells per row, cell texts as parsed, and the cells of the first row flagged as column headers.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Each `F>` line becomes one picture element whose caption text is the caption from that line.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Nothing else is added to the document.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'### 2. Unified batch CLI\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'`/home/user/project/rcp_convert.py`, run from `/home/user/project` as\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'```\n'[39m +
                [32m'python rcp_convert.py --input-dir <INPUT_DIR> --output-dir <OUTPUT_DIR>\n'[39m +
                [32m'```\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'converts a mixed directory in one pass. Both options are required.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Inputs are the regular files **directly** inside `<INPUT_DIR>` whose name ends in `.rcp` or `.md`, processed in ascending file-name order. Subdirectories and every other file are ignored.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* `<OUTPUT_DIR>` is created when missing and receives:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * `markdown/<stem>.md` — the Markdown export of the converted document (leading/trailing whitespace is not significant);\n'[39m +
                [32m"  * `json/<stem>.json` — the document's JSON serialization, loadable back into an equivalent Docling document;\n"[39m +
                [32m"  * `chunks.jsonl` — one JSON object per line, produced by Docling's hierarchical chunking of each converted document, in chunker order, grouped per input file in the same ascending file-name order. Each object has exactly the keys `file` (input file name), `chunk_index` (integer, restarting at 0 for each input file), `headings` (list of strings, the chunk's heading path, empty when there is none), `text` (the chunk text) and `num_chars` (integer, the number of characters in `text`);\n"[39m +
                [32m'  * `summary.json` — a JSON object with exactly the keys `schema_version` (integer `1`), `counts` and `documents`. `counts` has exactly the keys `total`, `succeeded` and `failed` (integers). `documents` is a list, ordered by `file` ascending, with one object per input file having exactly the keys `file` (file name), `format` (`"rcp"` or `"md"`), `status` (`"success"` or `"failure"`), `num_headings` (number of section-heading elements in the converted document), `num_list_items` (number of list items), `num_tables`, `num_pictures`, `num_chunks` (number of chunk objects written for this file) and `sha256` (lowercase hex SHA-256 of the input file\'s bytes).\n'[39m +
                [32m'* A file that fails to convert gets `"status": "failure"`, zeros in all five numeric fields, no `markdown/` or `json/` output and no line in `chunks.jsonl`; the run still processes every other file.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* The last line printed on stdout is exactly `converted=<succeeded> failed=<failed> total=<total>`.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Exit code `0` when every input converted, `1` when at least one input failed to convert, `2` when `<INPUT_DIR>` does not exist or is not a directory (in that case nothing else is required). A Python traceback must never reach stderr.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Re-running the command with the same input and output directories must rewrite byte-identical output files.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'## Implementation Hints\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Project path: `/home/user/project`; both `rcp_plugin.py` and `rcp_convert.py` live directly in it, and `rcp_plugin` must be importable as a top-level module with `/home/user/project` on `sys.path`.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Command: `python rcp_convert.py --input-dir <INPUT_DIR> --output-dir <OUTPUT_DIR>`, run with `/home/user/project` as the working directory.\n'[39m +
                [32m"* Conversion, exports and chunking must be produced by Docling itself from the converted document; hand-written Markdown/JSON/chunk emitters that bypass Docling's conversion entry point are not acceptable.\n"[39m +
                [32m'* Everything must run offline on CPU.'[39m
            }

                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'### InputFormat Enum\n'[39m +
                    [32m'**File**: `/opt/app-root/lib/python3.12/site-packages/docling/datamodel/base_models.py` (lines ~95-120)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```python\n'[39m +
                    [32m'class InputFormat(str, Enum):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    DOCX = "docx"\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    PPTX = "pptx"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    PPT = "ppt"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    HTML = "html"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    IMAGE = "image"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    PDF = "pdf"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ASCIIDOC = "asciidoc"\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    CSV = "csv"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    XLSX = "xlsx"\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    ODS = "ods"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ODP = "odp"\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    XML_XBRL = "xml_xbrl"\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    DCLX = "dclx"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    METS_GBS = "mets_gbs"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    JSON_DOCLING = "json_docling"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    AUDIO = "audio"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    VIDEO = "video"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    VTT = "vtt"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    LATEX = "latex"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    EMAIL = "email"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    EPUB = "epub"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    BOXNOTE = "boxnote"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'### Format Detection Pipeline\n'[39m +
                    [32m'**File**: `/opt/app-root/lib/python3.12/site-packages/docling/datamodel/document.py`, method `_guess_format()` (line ~765)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'Detection happens in `_DocumentConversionInput.docs()`:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'1. **Extension-based early exit**: `.dclg`/`.dclg.xml` → `XML_DOCLANG`, `.dclx` → `DCLX`\n'[39m +
                    [32m'2. **MIME detection via `filetype` library** (libmagic-based)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'3. **Extension-based MIME fallback** via `_mime_from_extension()`\n'[39m +
                    [32m'4. **Office XML detection**: If MIME is `application/zip`, checks ZIP contents for `word/document.xml`, `xl/workbook.xml`, `ppt/presentation.xml`\n'[39m +
                    [32m'5. **METS-GBS detection**: If MIME is `application/gzip`, checks for METS XML inside tar\n'[39m +
                    [32m'6. **Content sniffing**: If MIME is `application/xml` or `application/xhtml+xml`, reads first 1024 bytes to detect XBRL, USPTO, JATS, DocLang via DOCTYPE/root element\n'[39m +
                    [32m'7. **Fallback to `text/plain`**: If nothing matches\n'[39m +
                    [32m'8. **Disambiguation via `_guess_from_content()`**: When multiple formats share a MIME type\n'[39m +
                    [32m'9. **MIME → Format mapping**: `MimeTypeToFormat` dict resolves the final format\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'### Format → Backend Mapping\n'[39m +
                    [32m'**File**: `/opt/app-root/lib/python3.12/site-packages/docling/datamodel/base_models.py` (lines ~140-200)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'Two key dictionaries control format recognition:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'- **`FormatToExtensions`**: Maps `InputFormat` → list of file extensions (e.g., `InputFormat.PDF: ["pdf"]`)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'- **`FormatToMimeType`**: Maps `InputFormat` → list of MIME types (e.g., `InputFormat.PDF: ["application/pdf"]`)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'- **`MimeTypeToFormat`**: Reverse mapping (auto-generated from `FormatToMimeType`)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'### Format → Pipeline Mapping\n'[39m +
                    [32m'**File**: `/opt/app-root/lib/python3.12/site-packages/docling/document_converter.py`, function `_get_default_option()` (line ~230)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'Each `InputFormat` is mapped to a `FormatOption` subclass that specifies:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'- `pipeline_cls`: The pipeline class (e.g., `SimplePipeline`, `StandardPdfPipeline`, `AsrPipeline`, `VideoPipeline`)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'- `backend`: The backend class (e.g., `MarkdownDocumentBackend`, `DoclingParseDocumentBackend`)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'- `backend_options`: Optional backend-specific options\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'### Key Architecture: Two Pipeline Types\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'1. **SimplePipeline** (`/opt/app-root/lib/python3.12/site-packages/docling/pipeline/simple_pipeline.py`):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'   - Used for declarative backends (HTML, Markdown, DOCX, PPTX, XLSX, CSV, etc.)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'   - Backend must extend `DeclarativeDocumentBackend` (from `abstract_backend.py`)\n'[39m +
                    [32m"   - Backend's `convert()` method returns a `DoclingDocument` directly\n"[39m +
                    [32m'   - Pipeline then runs enrichment (picture classification, description, chart extraction)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'2. **StandardPdfPipeline** (`/opt/app-root/lib/python3.12/site-packages/docling/pipeline/standard_pdf_pipeline.py`):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'   - Used for PDF and images\n'[39m +
                    [32m'   - Backend must extend `PaginatedDocumentBackend`\n'[39m +
                    [32m'   - Runs page-by-page: layout detection, OCR, table structure, reading order, assembly\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'---\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'## 4. Abstract Backend Classes\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'**File**: `/opt/app-root/lib/python3.12/site-packages/docling/backend/abstract_backend.py`\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```python\n'[39m +
                    [32m'class AbstractDocumentBackend(ABC):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @abstractmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def __init__(self, in_doc: InputDocument, path_or_stream: Union[BytesIO, Path], options: Optional[BaseBackendOptions] = None)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @abstractmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def is_valid(self) -> bool\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @classmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @abstractmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def supports_pagination(cls) -> bool\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def unload(self)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @classmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @abstractmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def supported_formats(cls) -> set[InputFormat]\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'class PaginatedDocumentBackend(AbstractDocumentBackend):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @abstractmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def page_count(self) -> int\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'class DeclarativeDocumentBackend(AbstractDocumentBackend):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @abstractmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def convert(self) -> DoclingDocument  # ← KEY METHOD for custom formats\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'To implement a custom format, you extend `DeclarativeDocumentBackend` and implement `convert()` → `DoclingDocument`.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'---\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'## 5. DoclingDocument Model — Building Documents Programmatically\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'**File**: `/opt/app-root/lib/python3.12/site-packages/docling_core/types/doc/document.py`\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'### Constructor\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```python\n'[39m +
                    [32m'DoclingDocument(name: str, origin: Optional[DocumentOrigin] = None)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'### Key `add_*` Methods\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```python\n'[39m +
                    [32m'# Title (level-0 heading, renders as "# Title")\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_title(text, orig=None, prov=None, parent=None, content_layer=None, formatting=None, hyperlink=None) → TitleItem\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'# Section header (level N, renders as "## Heading" for level=1, "### Heading" for level=2, etc.)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_heading(text, orig=None, level=1, prov=None, parent=None, content_layer=None, formatting=None, hyperlink=None) → SectionHeaderItem\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'# Generic text\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_text(label, text, orig=None, prov=None, parent=None, content_layer=None, formatting=None, hyperlink=None) → TextItem\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'# List item (must have ListGroup parent; auto-creates one if not provided)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_list_item(text, enumerated=False, marker=None, orig=None, prov=None, parent=None, content_layer=None, formatting=None, hyperlink=None) → ListItem\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'# Code block\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_code(text, code_language=None, orig=None, caption=None, prov=None, parent=None, content_layer=None, formatting=None, hyperlink=None) → CodeItem\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'# Formula/equation\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_formula(text, orig=None, prov=None, parent=None, content_layer=None, formatting=None, hyperlink=None) → FormulaItem\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'# Table\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_table(data: TableData, caption=None, prov=None, parent=None, label=DocItemLabel.TABLE, content_layer=None, annotations=None) → TableItem\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'# Picture/figure\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_picture(annotations=None, image=None, caption=None, prov=None, parent=None, content_layer=None) → PictureItem\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'# Groups\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_list_group(name="list", parent=None, ordered=False) → ListGroup\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_inline_group(parent=None) → InlineGroup\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_group(name="group", parent=None) → GroupItem\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'# Key-value items\n'[39m +
                    [32m'add_key_values(graph: GraphData, prov=None, parent=None) → KeyValueItem\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'### TableData Construction\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```python\n'[39m +
                    [32m'from docling_core.types.doc import TableData, TableCell\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'cells = [\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    TableCell(text="Header 1", row_span=1, col_span=1, \n'[39m +
                    [32m'              start_row_offset_idx=0, end_row_offset_idx=1,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'              start_col_offset_idx=0, end_col_offset_idx=1,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'              column_header=True),\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    TableCell(text="Header 2", row_span=1, col_span=1,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'              start_row_offset_idx=0, end_row_offset_idx=1,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'              start_col_offset_idx=1, end_col_offset_idx=2,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'              column_header=True),\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    TableCell(text="Value 1", row_span=1, col_span=1,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'              start_row_offset_idx=1, end_row_offset_idx=2,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'              start_col_offset_idx=0, end_col_offset_idx=1),\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    TableCell(text="Value 2", row_span=1, col_span=1,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'              start_row_offset_idx=1, end_row_offset_idx=2,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'              start_col_offset_idx=1, end_col_offset_idx=2),\n'[39m +
                    [32m']\n'[39m +
                    [32m'table_data = TableData(num_rows=2, num_cols=2, table_cells=cells)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'doc.add_table(data=table_data)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'### Provenance (optional but important for page references)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```python\n'[39m +
                    [32m'from docling_core.types.doc import ProvenanceItem, BoundingBox, CoordOrigin\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'prov = ProvenanceItem(\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    page_no=1,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    bbox=BoundingBox(l=0, t=0, r=100, b=20, coord_origin=CoordOrigin.BOTTOMLEFT),\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    charspan=(0, 10)  # optional\n'[39m +
                    [32m')\n'[39m +
                    [32m'doc.add_text(label=DocItemLabel.TEXT, text="Hello", prov=prov)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'```\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'---\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'doc.export_t'[39m... 7603 more characters

                    [32m'        HTTP download aborted for exceeding ``limits.max_file_size``. The normal\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        unavailable in that case, so the fields are set explicitly here.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        ``model_construct`` is the supported way to do that. Only fields that\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        differ from their declared defaults are passed; the rest (e.g.\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    @staticmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def _classify_source_error(exc: BaseException) -> InputRejection:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Map a source-resolution failure to an InputRejection.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        Splits HTTP status rejections the same way as the jobkit task path\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        (``_classify_http_status``): policy status codes map to POLICY, every\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        other status or transport failure maps to SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        ``response.status_code`` is read by duck-typing to avoid a ``requests``\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        import.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        response = getattr(exc, "response", None)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        status_code = getattr(response, "status_code", None)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if status_code in {401, 403, 404, 413, 415, 422}:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            return InputRejection(message=str(exc), category=FailureCategory.POLICY)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return InputRejection(\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            message=str(exc) or "Source document could not be reached.",\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            category=FailureCategory.SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        )\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m"        Reuses docling-core's ``resolve_remote_filename`` for URLs (the same\n"[39m +

                    [32m'        the query string dropped. Non-URL sources fall back to the path basename.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        try:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            http_url = TypeAdapter(AnyHttpUrl).validate_python(source)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        except ValidationError:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            return PurePath(source).name or source\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return resolve_remote_filename(http_url=http_url, response_headers={})\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'            if _DocumentConversionInput._has_doclang_extension(obj.name):\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    ref: "RefItem"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def _get_text(self, doc: Optional["DoclingDocument"] = None, **kwargs: Any) -> str:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        from docling_core.transforms.serializer.markdown import MarkdownDocSerializer\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if doc is not None:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            doc_serializer = kwargs.pop("doc_serializer", MarkdownDocSerializer(doc=doc))\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ser_res = doc_serializer.serialize(item=self.ref.resolve(doc=doc), **kwargs)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            return ser_res.text\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        else:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            return "<!-- rich cell -->"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'AnyTableCell = Annotated[\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    Union[RichTableCell, TableCell],\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    Field(union_mode="left_to_right"),\n'[39m +
                    [32m']\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'class Orientation(str, Enum):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    """Counter-clockwise rotation of a table on the page, in degrees.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m"    Follows the convention used by PIL/Pillow's ``Image.rotate``: positive\n"[39m +
                    [32m'    angles rotate the table counter-clockwise. ``ROT_0`` / ``ROT_180`` keep\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    rows running horizontally on the page; ``ROT_90`` / ``ROT_270`` turn\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    rows into vertical stripes.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ROT_0 = "rot_0"  # no rotation; row 0 at top, rows horizontal\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ROT_90 = "rot_90"  # 90° CCW; row 0 on the left, rows are vertical stripes\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ROT_180 = "rot_180"  # 180°; row 0 at bottom (upside-down), rows horizontal\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ROT_270 = "rot_270"  # 270° CCW (= 90° CW); row 0 on the right, rows are vertical stripes\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'class TableData(BaseModel):  # TBD\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    """BaseTableData."""\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    table_cells: list[AnyTableCell] = []\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    num_rows: int = 0\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    num_cols: int = 0\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    orientation: Orientation = Orientation.ROT_0\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @computed_field  # type: ignore\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @property\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def grid(\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ) -> list[list[TableCell]]:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Grid."""\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        # Initialise empty table data grid (only empty cells)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        table_data = [\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            [\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                TableCell(\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    text="",\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    start_row_offset_idx=i,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    end_row_offset_idx=i + 1,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    start_col_offset_idx=j,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    end_col_offset_idx=j + 1,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                )\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                for j in range(self.num_cols)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ]\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            for i in range(self.num_rows)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        ]\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        # Overwrite cells in table data for which there is actual cell content.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        for cell in self.table_cells:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            for i in range(\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                min(cell.start_row_offset_idx, self.num_rows),\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                min(cell.end_row_offset_idx, self.num_rows),\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                for j in range(\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    min(cell.start_col_offset_idx, self.num_cols),\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    min(cell.end_col_offset_idx, self.num_cols),\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                ):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    table_data[i][j] = cell\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return table_data\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def remove_rows(self, indices: list[int], doc: Optional["DoclingDocument"] = None) -> list[list[TableCell]]:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Remove rows from the table by their indices.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :param indices: list[int]: A list of indices of the rows to remove. (Starting from 0)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :return: list[list[TableCell]]: A list representation of the removed rows as lists of TableCell objects.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if not indices:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            return []\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        indices = sorted(indices, reverse=True)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        refs_to_remove = []\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        all_removed_cells = []\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        for row_index in indices:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            if row_index < 0 or row_index >= self.num_rows:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                raise IndexError(\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    f"Row index {row_index} is out of bounds for the current number of rows {self.num_rows}."\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                )\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            start_idx = row_index * self.num_cols\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            end_idx = start_idx + self.num_cols\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            removed_cells = self.table_cells[start_idx:end_idx]\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            for cell in removed_cells:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                if isinstance(cell, RichTableCell):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    refs_to_remove.append(cell.ref)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # Remove the cells from the table\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            self.table_cells = self.table_cells[:start_idx] + self.table_cells[end_idx:]\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # Update the number of rows\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            self.num_rows -= 1\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # Reassign row offset indices for existing cells\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            for index, cell in enumerate(self.table_cells):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                new_index = index // self.num_cols\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                cell.start_row_offset_idx = new_index\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                cell.end_row_offset_idx = new_index + 1\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            all_removed_cells.append(removed_cells)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if refs_to_remove:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            if doc is None:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                _logger.warning(\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    "When table contains rich cells, `doc` argument must be provided, "\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    "otherwise rich cell content will be left dangling."\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                )\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            else:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                doc._delete_items(refs_to_remove)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return all_removed_cells\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def pop_row(self, doc: Optional["DoclingDocument"] = None) -> list[TableCell]:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Remove and return the last row from the table.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :returns: list[TableCell]: A list of TableCell objects representing the popped row.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if self.num_rows == 0:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            raise IndexError("Cannot pop from an empty table.")\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return self.remove_row(self.num_rows - 1, doc=doc)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def remove_row(self, row_index: int, doc: Optional["DoclingDocument"] = None) -> list[TableCell]:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Remove a row from the table by its index.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        :returns: list[TableCell]: A list of TableCell objects representing the removed row.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return self.remove_rows([row_index], doc=doc)[0]\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def insert_rows(self, row_index: int, rows: list[list[str]], after: bool = False) -> None:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Insert multiple new rows from a list of lists of strings before/after a specific index in the table.\n'[39m +

            [32m'\n'[39m +
            [32m'# Committing changes with git\n'[39m +

            [32m'- NEVER skip hooks (--no-verify, --no-gpg-sign, etc) unless the user explicitly requests it\n'[39m +

Instead, after hook failure, fix the issue, re-stage, and create a NEW commit\n'[39m +
            [32m'- When staging files, prefer adding specific files by name rather than using "git add -A" or "git add .", which can accidentally include sensitive files (.env, credentials) or large binaries\n'[39m +
